# ROADMAP 130: length give-aways in Hungarian choice exercises — worksheet
#
# One entry per exercise. Identical copies (lesson + consolidation file) are
# ONE entry; `files:` lists every copy and every copy gets the same change.
# `lesson:` is the lesson the exercise belongs to: read it (and the grammar
# it references) before deciding, so you know what the learner has met.
#
# Fill in `decision:` for EVERY entry in a block BEFORE you edit that block,
# with one of:
#   shorten: <the new correct option, in full>
#   match: <each new wrong option, in full, separated by " || ">
#          (with 2 options there is one wrong option, so one text only)
#   log                -> you are not certain; add it to hu-review-questions.txt,
#                         change nothing
# then one short line `why:` saying why the result has exactly one right
# answer AND why the Hungarian of every option you wrote is correct (or, for
# a wrong option, wrong for exactly the reason you intend). An empty decision
# means the entry was not reviewed. The reviewer diffs every changed option
# against this file.


########## Block 1: hu a1 + a2 (70 exercises) ##########

### a1-08-dialogue-1   teaches: ['a1-greetings-basic-interaction-vocab']
files: content/hu/exercises/a1/a1-08-ex.json
lesson: content/hu/lessons/a1/a1-08.json
Anna: Hogy vagy?
Meg: _____
* Jól vagyok, köszönöm. És te?
- Bocsánat!
decision: match: Viszontlátásra, szép napot!
why: A farewell wishing a nice day does not answer 'Hogy vagy?'; natural polite A1 vocabulary of matching length (27 vs 28 chars).

### a1-09-dialogue-1   teaches: ['a1-greetings-basic-interaction-vocab']
files: content/hu/exercises/a1/a1-09-ex.json

Mariann: Szia, Meg, örülök, hogy újra látlak, hogy vagy?
Meg: _____
* Nem értem. Még egyszer, légyszi.
- Szívesen!
decision: log
why: The marked option does not answer the greeting/inquiry 'hogy vagy?'; known defect noted in hu-review-feedback-a1-a2.md § 2.

### a1-09-practice-5  (same exercise also as: a1-10-consolidation-16)   teaches: ['a1-greetings-basic-interaction-vocab']

* Mondom még egyszer, lassabban.
- Szívesen.
decision: match: Nagyon szívesen, nincs mit.
why: Replying 'You are welcome, don't mention it' does not answer 'Mit mondtál?'; standard polite A1 phrase matching length (27 vs 30 chars).

### a1-27-practice-1   teaches: ['van-possessive-to-have']
files: content/hu/exercises/a1/a1-27-ex.json
lesson: content/hu/lessons/a1/a1-27.json
How do you say 'I have' in Hungarian?
* van + possessive suffix
- habok
- vagyok
decision: match: habok + accusative suffix || vagyok + possessive suffix
why: Preserves distractor_skills for option 2 (van-zero-copula) by keeping 1st person copula vagyok; balanced grammatical descriptors (25, 26 vs 23 chars).

Why does szoba lengthen its final -a to -á before the possessive suffix?
* vowel-final stems shift shape slightly before the suffix
- it never changes
- it's a random exception
decision: match: vowel-final stems never change when suffixes attach || it is a completely irregular spelling exception
why: Expands the two false grammatical claims into full parallel explanation clauses of balanced length (49, 47 vs 56 chars).

### a1-68-dialogue-1   teaches: ['spatial-questions-hol-hova']

### a1-85-practice-5   teaches: ['a1-frequency-word-order-vocab']
files: content/hu/exercises/a1/a1-85-ex.json

### a2-180-consolidation-14   teaches: ['essive-formal-kent']
files: content/hu/exercises/a2/a2-180-consolidation-ex.json
lesson: content/hu/lessons/a2/a2-180-consolidation.json
What is the difference between 'például' and 'példaként'?
* 'például' is 'for example', 'példaként' is 'as an example'
- 'például' is a verb
- They mean the opposite
decision: match: 'például' is an inflected verb, while 'példaként' is a noun || They have completely opposite meanings in everyday speech
why: Both false grammatical/semantic explanations are expanded into full English sentences of matching length (59, 57 vs 58 chars).

### a2-180-intro-1   teaches: ['essive-formal-kent']
files: content/hu/exercises/a2/a2-180-ex.json
lesson: content/hu/lessons/a2/a2-180.json
What is the difference between 'Tanár vagyok' and 'Tanárként dolgozom'?
* 'Tanár vagyok' states identity, 'tanárként dolgozom' describes one's current role
- They mean completely opposite things
- There is no difference
decision: match: 'Tanár vagyok' and 'tanárként dolgozom' mean completely opposite things || There is no difference between them in either meaning or grammar usage
why: Expands the two false contrastive claims into full clear English sentences of matching length (71, 70 vs 81 chars).

### a2-181-practice-4   teaches: ['formal-address-on']
files: content/hu/exercises/a2/a2-181-ex.json
lesson: content/hu/lessons/a2/a2-181.json
On the tram, you offer your seat to an elderly woman. What is best to say?
* Parancsoljon, le tetszik ülni?
- Ülj le ide!
- Itt a helyem.
decision: match: Ülj le ide gyorsan a helyemre! || Itt a helyem, és nem állok fel.
why: Option 1 is an inappropriate informal command (violating formal address); option 2 refuses to yield seat; lengths match (30, 31 vs 30 chars).

### a2-182-practice-4   teaches: ['a2-sociocultural-pragmatics-customs-vocab']
files: content/hu/exercises/a2/a2-182-ex.json

In a formal shop, how does the clerk ask 'Can I help you?'
* Parancsoljon, miben segíthetek?
- Mit akarsz?
- Mit hoztál?
decision: match: Mit akarsz venni nálunk a boltban? || Mit hoztál ma ide nekem a boltba?
why: Both wrong options are inappropriately informal/rude questions that a clerk would not ask; lengths match (34, 33 vs 31 chars).

### a2-212-check-1   teaches: ['a2-banking-vocab']
files: content/hu/exercises/a2/a2-212-ex.json
lesson: content/hu/lessons/a2/a2-212.json
What should you do immediately if the ATM does not dispense cash after debiting your account?
* Azonnal fel kell hívni a bank telefonos ügyfélszolgálatát.

What is the exact difference between 'olvastat' and 'olvasgat'?
* 'olvastat' is causative ('makes someone read'), while 'olvasgat' is frequentative/attenuative ('reads casually at leisure').
- Both mean 'reads casually at leisure'.
- 'olvasgat' is causative, while 'olvastat' is frequentative.

- Az autópályák melletti pihenőhelyek hiányát a tranzitútvonalakon.

why:
